### Tool call: browser_evaluate
- Args
```json
{
  "function": "() => ({roads:document.getElementById('road-count').textContent,hint:document.getElementById('toast').textContent})"
}
```
- Result
```json
{
  "result": "{\n  \"roads\": \"20\",\n  \"hint\": \"道路已连接\"\n}",
  "code": "await page.evaluate('() => ({roads:document.getElementById(\\'road-count\\').textContent,hint:document.getElementById(\\'toast\\').textContent})');"
}
```

### Tool call: browser_click
- Args
```json
{
  "target": "[data-tool=\"source\"]"
}
```
- Result
```json
{
```

### Tool call: browser_evaluate
- Args
```json
{
  "function": "() => ({running:document.getElementById('map').dataset.running,network:document.getElementById('network-note').textContent,lights:document.querySelectorAll('[data-light-ew]').length})"
}
```
- Result
```json
{
  "result": "{\n  \"running\": \"true\",\n  \"network\": \"✓ 1 个入口均有可达路线 · 1 个出口\",\n  \"lights\": 1\n}",
  "code": "await page.evaluate('() => ({running:document.getElementById(\\'map\\').dataset.running,network:document.getElementById(\\'network-note\\').textContent,lights:document.querySelectorAll(\\'[data-light-ew]\\').length})');"
}
```
